## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> look at the bed

The thin bed, covered in meager bedding, catches your gaze as you sit upright, awakened. Despite the grime clinging to your skin, the sight of the bed is as plain as the barred door that seals your cell. The cup and book, stationed across the room, remain unchanged, offering no escape or company. The silence hangs heavy, unbroken by any staff, leaving you alone in this cold, grimy cell.

## Turn 2
> look at the cup

In the dimly lit cell, your gaze lingers on the cup beside the bed, half-full of water. The cup remains unchanged, the water still sits within it, undisturbed by your query. The dampness of the room seems more palpable now, a constant reminder of your surroundings. Your words, though spoken, seem to lose some of their intended weight in this strange place.

## Turn 3
> look at the book

The book, your gaze, a bridge over a chasm of incomprehension. The first page, a blur, the second illegible. By the third, the world shifts. Torchlight dances on damp stone as the tunnel opens into a shallow alcove. A low table of unfinished rock sits against the left wall, six sealed caskets resting on it in a neat row. Five lids are unmarked, but the sixth bears your name, etched cleanly into the wood, as if the carver knew you would come. The air here is still; the tunnel ahead beckons into deeper darkness. Six caskets, one for you, the named one, in an alcove, the beginning of another chapter.

## Turn 4
> look at the door

In the dimly lit tunnel, the torchlight dances across damp stone, illuminating the entrance to a small alcove. The chamber is sparse, with a low table of unfinished rock against the left wall. Six caskets rest atop it, each secured with iron straps and a simple latch. Five of the caskets remain unadorned, but the sixth bears a name that catches your eye. Your gaze lingers, taking in the scene as time slowly ticks by. You notice the stone table and the six caskets, including the one with your name etched upon it.

## Turn 5
> look at the slit

The torchlight flickers, casting a spectral dance upon the damp stone as you approach the tunnel's end. The opening reveals a shallow alcove, within which rests a stone table against the left wall. Six sealed caskets, each bound with iron straps and a simple latch, occupy the table in a neat row. Five of them remain unmarked, while the sixth bears your name.

Your eyes trace over the caskets, taking in the details of the stone table and the simple containers that lie upon it.

The damp, musty air of the tunnel gives way to a stillness that speaks of the ancient origins of these artifacts. As the torchlight catches the iron straps, the caskets seem to whisper secrets long forgotten, their silence broken only by the steady ticking of the passage of time.

## Turn 6
> examine myself

In the dimly lit tunnel, the torchlight flickers, illuminating a shallow alcove. The damp stone catches the light, reflecting a dance of shadows on the wall. A low, unfinished stone table sits against the left wall, and upon it, six sealed caskets rest in a neat row. Five of them are unadorned, their simplicity stark against the rough stone. The sixth casket, however, bears a name that catches your eye. You find yourself noticing the six caskets, the named casket, and the stone table.

> wait

In the dimly lit cell, the observation slit in the door creaks open, revealing a figure on the other side. The person outside speaks, a series of short, repetitive sounds that, despite their incomprehensibility, carry a clear message: move away from the door. Yet, as the words echo, the room remains unyielding, the walls refusing to budge an inch.

A soft knocking sound, distinct and insistent, filters through the silence, not from the corridor you were expecting, but from the very pages of the book beneath your thumb. The cell, in its stoic solitude, continues its eternal vigil, the knocking echoing with a strange dissonance.

## Turn 13
> look around

The corridor continues, its familiar bolts echoing the cell door behind. Your hands are still bound, a constant reminder of the institution's control. You strain to look around, but your eyes meet the same cold walls, the same silent orderlies. The one who seems large and silent, practical, leads. The younger one, tense and rule-bound, follows close behind. The air is thick, carrying an unspoken tension that lingers under your skin, leaving it feeling unclean enough to notice. The words you speak seem to thin before they leave your lips. The corridor moves on, its pace relentless, as you are led deeper into the institution.

## Turn 14
> wait

In the sterile, echoing washroom, the staff lead you to a basin filled with water. Soap with a medicinal scent is nearby. The larger staff member maintains a practiced silence, while the younger one seems tense and rule-bound. The air is thick with unspoken tension.

The basin, a constant in this routine, does not speak its purpose. The water, cold and clear, waits for you. The soap, too, remains silent, its purpose clear despite its inability to voice it. The staff stand by, their patience worn thin.

You wait, the silence heavy between you and them. The basin, the soap, the staff - all seem to remind you of the unspoken rules, the unspoken expectations. You stand there, hands bound, unable to fill the silence. The staff do not intervene, waiting for the inevitable - the wash to begin. The tension hangs in the air, a palpable reminder of the procedure to come.

--- J. NARRATOR OUTPUT ---
In the sterile, echoing washroom, the staff lead you to a basin filled with water. Soap with a medicinal scent is nearby. The larger staff member maintains a practiced silence, while the younger one seems tense and rule-bound. The air is thick with unspoken tension.

The basin, a constant in this routine, does not speak its purpose. The water, cold and clear, waits for you. The soap, too, remains silent, its purpose clear despite its inability to voice it. The staff stand by, their patience worn thin.

You wait, the silence heavy between you and them. The basin, the soap, the staff - all seem to remind you of the unspoken rules, the unspoken expectations. You stand there, hands bound, unable to fill the silence. The staff do not intervene, waiting for the inevitable - the wash to begin. The tension hangs in the air, a palpable reminder of the procedure to come.

A staff member, large and silent, watches you intently, while another, younger and more tense, stands rigidly beside them. ## Turn 18
> wait

In the cell, the same as before. The moved objects remain unmoved, a testament to the inertia of your actions. A sense of cleanliness, accompanied by a heavier fatigue, envelopes you. The gnawing hunger eases a little.

The bed lies beneath you, the cup beside it, a book face-down on the floor. The door, absent of a handle on this side, remains a barrier. A narrow slit, eye-level, offers a view to the world outside. The taste of stale air fills your mouth.

## Turn 22
> wait

In the institutional corridor, the rhythm of footsteps echoes as you are escorted toward a questioning room. The air is heavy with the weight of expectations, the staff members flanking you, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, both adhering to the rules that govern this place.

Your hands are firm on your own arms, a testament to your desire to remain still. Yet, as you wait, words come out thinner than you intend, a physical contrast to the unspoken tension that fills the corridor.

Suddenly, one of the staff members shows you a word, a name. Yours? The unease under your ribs deepens, a tangible reminder of the game you are playing within this institution. The corridor remains unchanged, the same bolts still adorning the doors, the same pace of the institution. But now, you are faced with a new reality, one that demands a response.

> wait

In the dimly lit facility, the atmosphere remains tense as you find yourself facing two staff members. One, controlled and kind, the other, large and practical. The younger staff member taps on the table, placing two figures in front of you - older and younger, perhaps family?

A smaller figure appears, a brother, maybe. The room carries a peculiar scent, and a name echoes in your mind, triggering vivid memories of childhood. Cold air and wood under your feet. Someone's hand. The memory arrives uninvited.

They persist, asking about the events that transpired when you died. If you claim not to remember dying, they press on, describing darkness, waking, warmth, pain, voices, light.

## Turn 28
> wait

In the facility's interview room, a controlled, professional staff member and a large, practical one continue their interrogation. The air is heavy with tension, and the recent beats have been a series of questions and memories, some vivid, some disconcerting.

You wait, your breath catching in your chest as a faint smell of wet rope fills the room, tightening your chest as if the water is physically there. A warmth and sunlight seep into your senses, accompanied by the images of clean bedding, food, and running water. It's unclear whether these are memories, dreams, or contrived environments.

The questioning resumes, focusing on the events surrounding your death. You hesitate, unsure of what to say, words coming out thinner than intended. Unease sits under your ribs as they wait for your response.

## Turn 30
> wait

The sun dips low, casting long shadows on the stones of the jetty. A question hangs in the air, a stark contrast to the quiet. "Do you know it?" they ask, referring to a place you can't quite place.

Unease settles under your ribs as you ponder their query. Words come out thinner than intended, and the silence stretches. The staff member who seems controlled, collared, professionally kind, waits patiently, their gaze steady. The other, a figure of silence and practicality, shifts position, their gaze lingering on you.

The conversation veers back to the other place. The heat, the cold, the foul air, the noise, the thirst. The conviction that it will never end. Fear of that place settles somewhere you cannot file away. The questions continue, a persistent echo of the unknown.

The sun dips low, casting long shadows on the stones of the jetty. A question hangs in the air, a stark contrast to the quiet. "Do you know it?" they ask, referring to a place you can't quite place.

The conversation veers back to the other place. The heat, the cold, the foul air, the noise, the thirst. The conviction that it will never end. Fear of that place settles somewhere you cannot file away. The questions continue, a persistent echo of the unknown.

## Turn 31
> wait

In the heart of the facility, amidst the chill of winter, an unspoken question hangs heavy. You find yourself ensconced in an interview, flanked by two staff members - one controlled, collared, and professionally kind, the other large, silent, and practical. Their gazes are steady, their demeanor unyielding.

The air is thick with the scent of water, a reminder of a past you cannot quite grasp. Your chest constricts, the memory of drowning gnawing at you. The staff members wait, their focus unwavering, seeking answers to the mystery that surrounds you.

You try to speak, but the words feel thin, insubstantial, as if they are slipping away before they reach your audience. The staff member who seems controlled, collared, and professionally kind looks at you, expectant, waiting for a response to their question: "Winter. A drowning. Did that happen?"

Your response, however, remains uninterpretable
